```python
import numpy as np
import time

np.seterr(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore")


def normalize_nonnegative(v):
    """Normalize a 1D array to a nonnegative probability vector."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    return v / s


def fast_convolve(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    if C.size:
        mn = float(C.min())
        if mn < 0.0:
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    return C


def score_normalized(a):
    """Score of a normalized nonnegative sequence: 2*n*max(conv(a,a))."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * float(c.max())


def exact_score(a):
    """Use the official evaluator when available, otherwise fast score."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        sc = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return score_normalized(a)


def symmetrize(a):
    """Make a sequence palindromic."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    return normalize_nonnegative(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))


def interpolate_seq(f, new_n):
    """Linearly interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / int(new_n)
    if old_n == 1:
        return norm = np.full(int(new_n), max(float(f[0]), 0.0))
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n))
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, f)
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def make_power_left(n, alpha=0.5, eps=1.0):
    """Left-edge singularity: (i + eps)^(-alpha)."""
    x = np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + eps
    a = x ** (-alpha)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_power_left_decay(n, alpha=0.5, gamma=0.0, eps=1.0):
    """Left-edge singularity with exponential decay toward the right."""
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = i + eps
    if n > 1:
        pos = i / float(n - 1)
    else:
        pos = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = x ** (-alpha) * np.exp(-gamma * pos)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_power_both(n, alpha=0.25, beta=0.25, eps=1.0):
    """Two-edge singularity combination."""
    n = int(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    xl = i + eps
    xr = (n - 1) - i + eps
    a = xl ** (-alpha) + xr ** (-beta)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_gaussian(n, center=0.5, sigma=0.18):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - center) / sigma) ** 2)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_bell(n, width=0.55):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    t = np.clip(np.abs(x - 0.5) / (width / 2.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    a = 1.0 - t ** 4
    return normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(a, 0.0))


def make_bimodal(n, c1=0.25, c2=0.75, sigma=0.08):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / sigma) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / sigma) ** 2)
    return normalize_nonnegative(a)


def make_mixture(a, b, w):
    return normalize_nonnegative(float(w) * a + (1.0 - float(w)) * b)


def project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(float(v[0]), 0.0)], dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def exponent_weights(c, p):
    """Normalized weights proportional to (c / max(c))^p."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
    r = c / m
    if p <= 1.0:
        w = r
    else:
        w = np.power(r, float(p))
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / sw
    return w


def direct_gradient(a, indices, weights):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights[k] * (a*a)[k], using only supplied indices.
    Includes the exact factor 2 from d(A^2)/da.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx_arr_full = np.arange(indices.size)
    for i in idx_arr_full:
        k = int(indices[i])
        wi = float(weights[i])
        if wi <= 1e-300:
            continue
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k)
        if je < js:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(js, je + 1)
        g[js:je + 1] += 2.0 * wi * a[k - idx]
    return g


def strong_gradient(a, w):
    """Full weighted gradient of sum_k w[k]*(a*a)[k] via FFT correlation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 1:
        ww = float(w[0]) if w.size else 0.0
        return np.array([2.0 * float(a[0]) * ww], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(L, w.size)
        tmp[:m] = w[:m]
        w = tmp
    needed = n + L - 1
    N = 1 << (needed - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:needed]
    start = n - 1
    stop = 2 * n - 1
    if stop > conv.size:
        stop = conv.size
    corr = conv[start:stop][::-1]
    if corr.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, corr.size)
        tmp[:m] = corr[:m]
        corr = tmp
    return 2.0 * corr


def weighted_gradient(a, w, active_threshold=64):
    """Choose direct or FFT gradient depending on support of weights."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    active = np.nonzero(w > 1e-14)[0]
    if active.size <= active_threshold:
        return direct_gradient(a, active, w[active])
    return strong_gradient(a, w)


def make_mul_candidate(a, gc, step):
    """Multiplicative updated candidate on the simplex."""
    lu = -step * gc
    lu = np.clip(lu, -50.0, 50.0)
    cand = a * np.exp(lu)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return cand / s


def make_add_candidate(a, gc, step):
    """Additive update projected onto the simplex."""
    cand = a - step * gc
    cand = project_simplex(cand)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-15)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    return cand / s


def smooth_mirror(a0, time_limit, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0, eta_init=0.8,
                  verbose=False, label=""):
    """
    Multiplicative mirror descent with a p-smooth max surrogate.
    Uses exact gradient of the surrogate and adaptive p/step/restarts.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a.size
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n == 0:
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    p = p_start
    eta = eta_init
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        w = exponent_weights(c, p)
        g = weighted_gradient(a, w, 64)
        gc = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(gc, gc)))

        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(98765 + it)
            a = normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.001, 0.0))
            no_improve += 10
            continue

        base = eta / gnorm
        if base > 0.1:
            base = 0.1

        H = float(np.dot(w, c))
        chosen = None
        chosen_M = M
        accepted = False

        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            step = base * fac
            if step <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = make_mul_candidate(a, gc, step)
            cc = fast_convolve(cand)
            cm = float(cc.max())
            ch = float(np.dot(w, cc))
            if cm < M - 1e-15:
                chosen = cand
                chosen_M = cm
                accepted = True
                if cm < M - 1e-6:
                    break
            if ch < H * (1.0 - 1e-6) and cm < best_M * 1.05:
                chosen = cand
                chosen_M = cm
                accepted = True
                if cm < M - 1e-6:
                    break

        if accepted and chosen is not None:
            a = chosen
            c = fast_convolve(a)
            M = chosen_M
            if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta *= 0.5

        if eta < 1e-9:
            eta = eta_init
            no_improve += 50

        if no_improve > 40:
            p = min(p * 1.25, p_max * 4.0)
            eta = 0.5 * eta_init
            if no_improve > 200:
                p = p_start
                eta = eta_init
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 160:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(13579 + it)
            scale = 0.001 + 0.004 * min(float(no_improve) / 2000.0, 1.0)
            a = best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale
            a = normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(a, 0.0))
            p = p_start
            eta = eta_init
            no_improve = 0

        if it % 80 == 0:
            a_sym = symmetrize(a)
            c_sym = fast_convolve(a_sym)
            Msym = float(c_sym.max())
            if Msym < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = Msym
                best_a = a_sym.copy()
            if Msym < M:
                a = a_sym.copy()
                c = c_sym
                M = Msym
                if no_improve > 0:
                    no_improve -= 10

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"[smooth {label}] it={it} M={M:.10f} best={best_M:.10f} p={p:.2f} eta={eta:.3e}")

    return best_a, 2.0 * float(best_a.size) * best_M


def additive_mirror(a0, time_limit, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0, eta_init=0.6,
                    verbose=False, label=""):
    """
    Euclidean projected gradient / rectilinear flow with p-smooth max surrogate.
    Different geometry from multiplicative descent; useful for escaping local traps.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a.size
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n == 0:
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    p = p_start
    eta = eta_init
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        w = exponent_weights(c, p)
        g = weighted_gradient(a, w, 64)
        gc = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(gc, gc)))

        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(24680 + it)
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.001)
            no_improve += 10
            continue

        base = eta / gnorm
        if base > 0.02:
            base = 0.02

        H = float(np.dot(w, c))
        chosen = None
        chosen_M = M
        accepted = False

        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            step = base * fac
            if step <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = make_add_candidate(a, gc, step)
            cc = fast_convolve(cand)
            cm = float(cc.max())
            ch = float(np.dot(w, cc))
            if cm < M - 1e-15:
                chosen = cand
                chosen_M = cm
                accepted = True
                if cm < M - 1e-6:
                    break
            if ch < H * (1.0 - 1e-6) and cm < best_M * 1.05:
                chosen = cand
                chosen_M = cm
                accepted = True
                if cm < M - 1e-6:
                    break

        if accepted and chosen is not None:
            a = chosen
            c = fast_convolve(a)
            M = chosen_M
            if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta *= 0.5

        if eta < 1e-9:
            eta = eta_init
            no_improve += 50

        if no_improve > 40:
            p = min(p * 1.25, p_max * 4.0)
            eta = 0.5 * eta_init
            if no_improve > 200:
                p = p_start
                eta = eta_init
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 160:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(112233 + it)
            scale = 0.001 + 0.004 * min(float(no_improve) / 2000.0, 1.0)
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale)
            p = p_start
            eta = eta_init
            no_improve = 0

        if it % 80 == 0:
            a_sym = symmetrize(a)
            c_sym = fast_convolve(a_sym)
            Msym = float(c_sym.max())
            if Msym < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = Msym
                best_a = a_sym.copy()
            if Msym < M:
                a = a_sym.copy()
                c = c_sym
                M = Msym
                if no_improve > 0:
                    no_improve -= 10

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"[additive {label}] it={it} M={M:.10f} best={best_M:.10f} p={p:.2f} eta={eta:.3e}")

    return best_a, 2.0 * float(best_a.size) * best_M


def newton_flatten(a0, time_limit, reg_frac=1e-7, verbose=False, label=""):
    """
    Damped polynomial Newton step toward more uniform coefficients of A^2.
    Linearizes (A+d)^2 - A^2 = 2 A d and accepts only improvements.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a.size
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n == 0:
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    uniform = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        r = uniform - c
        size = 2 * n - 1
        N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        R = np.fft.rfft(r, N)

        denom = 2.0 * A
        denom_abs2 = np.abs(denom) ** 2
        max_abs2 = float(np.max(denom_abs2)) if denom_abs2.size else 0.0
        reg = reg_frac * max_abs2 + 1e-16
        D = R * np.conj(denom) / (denom_abs2 + reg)
        d = np.fft.irfft(D, N)[:n]

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        amax = float(np.max(a))
        if not np.isfinite(amax) or amax <= 0.0:
            amax = 1.0 / float(n)
        scale = max(amax, 1.0 / float(n))
        d = np.clip(d, -scale, scale)

        improved = False
        for lam in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            cand = normalize_nonnegative(a + lam * d)
            cc = fast_convolve(cand)
            cm = float(cc.max())
            if cm < M - 1e-14:
                a = cand
                M = cm
                no_improve = 0
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            for lam in (0.03, 0.01):
                cand = normalize_nonnegative(a - lam * d)
                cc = fast_convolve(cand)
                cm = float(cc.max())
                if cm < M - 1e-14:
                    a = cand
                    M = cm
                    no_improve = 0
                    break
            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 18:
                    rng = np.random.default_rng(777001 + it)
                    a = normalize_nonnegative(np.maximum(best_a + rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.001, 0.0))
                    no_improve = 0

        if it % 100 == 0:
            a_sym = symmetrize(a)
            c_sym = fast_convolve(a_sym)
            Msym = float(c_sym.max())
            if Msym < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = Msym
                best_a = a_sym.copy()
            if Msym < M:
                a = a_sym.copy()
                M = Msym

        if verbose and it % 100 == 0:
            print(f"[newton {label}] it={it} M={M:.10f} best={best_M:.10f}")

    return best_a, 2.0 * float(best_a.size) * best_M


def coordinate_refine(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    """
    Targeted coordinate refinement. Each coordinate is changed while rescaling the
    rest; coordinates are selected by current peak sensitivity plus mass.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a.size
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n == 0:
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_convolve(a).max())
    stall = 0
    max_stall = 18 if n <= 1024 else 8
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if n <= 1024:
            idxs = np.arange(n)
        else:
            idxs = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        if n > 1024:
            c = fast_convolve(a)
            K = min(10, c.size)
            if K >= c.size:
                peak_idx = np.arange(c.size)
            else:
                peak_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            wt = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            wt[peak_idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
            gg = direct_gradient(a, peak_idx, wt[peak_idx])
            gg = gg - float(np.mean(gg))
            nt = min(36, n)
            top = np.argsort(gg)[-nt:]
            bot = np.argsort(gg)[:max(1, nt // 3)]
            p = a / float(a.sum())
            rand = np.unique(rng.choice(n, size=nt, replace=False, p=p))
            idxs = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, bot, rand)))

        improved = False
        for j in idxs:
            old = float(a[j])
            if old < 1e-9 or old > 0.95:
                continue
            rest = 1.0 - old
            if rest < 1e-8:
                continue
            for mult in (0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.3):
                new = old * mult
                if new < 0.0 or new >= 1.0:
                    continue
                change = new - old
                factor = 1.0 - change / rest
                if factor <= 0.0:
                    continue
                cand = a * factor
                cand[j] = new
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                ss = float(cand.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                cand = cand / ss
                cm = float(fast_convolve(cand).max())
                if cm < best_M - 1e-14:
                    a = cand
                    best_M = cm
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > max_stall:
                break

        if verbose and stall % 5 == 0:
            print(f"[coord {label}] stalls={stall} best={2.0*best_a.size*best_M:.10f}")

    return best_a, 2.0 * float(best_a.size) * best_M


def update_best(seq, best_score, best_a):
    """Update best based on fast exact-equivalent score for normalized sequences."""
    if seq is None:
        return best_score, best_a
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return best_score, best_a
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return best_score, best_a
    sc = score_normalized(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_a = a / s
    return best_score, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Hybrid optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

    Strategy:
    - Seed from the parent construction and diverse analytic families.
    - Main multiplicative mirror descent with exact all-peak gradients.
    - Explore longer lengths.
    - Additive projected flow for different geometry.
    - Damped polynomial Newton flatten toward uniform square coefficients.
    - Targeted coordinate refinement.
    - Final polished mirror pass and symmetrization.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent = None
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if par is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and p.sum() > 1e-10:
                parent = normalize_nonnegative(p)
                sc = score_normalized(parent)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = parent.copy()
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    base_lengths = [768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096, 8192]
    if parent is not None:
        bl = int(parent.size)
        if 2 <= bl <= 8192:
            base_lengths.append(bl)
        if bl > 4096 and bl <= 8192:
            base_lengths.append(8192)

    unique_lengths = []
    for L in base_lengths:
        Li = int(L)
        if 2 <= Li <= 8192 and Li not in unique_lengths:
            unique_lengths.append(Li)
    unique_lengths.sort()

    top_candidates = []

    for n in unique_lengths:
        seqs = []
        if parent is not None:
            pn = interpolate_seq(parent, n)
            seqs.append(pn)
            seqs.append(symmetrize(pn))

        p1 = make_power_left(n, 0.5)
        p048 = make_power_left(n, 0.48)
        seqs.append(p1)
        seqs.append(p048)
        seqs.append(make_power_left_decay(n, 0.5, 0.3))
        seqs.append(make_power_left_decay(n, 0.5, 0.7))
        seqs.append(make_power_both(n, 0.25, 0.25))
        seqs.append(make_gaussian(n, 0.5, 0.18))
        seqs.append(make_gaussian(n, 0.5, 0.28))
        seqs.append(make_bell(n, 0.55))
        seqs.append(make_bimodal(n, 0.25, 0.75, 0.07))
        seqs.append(make_mixture(p1, make_gaussian(n, 0.5, 0.25), 0.85))
        seqs.append(make_mixture(p048, make_bell(n, 0.6), 0.7))

        for seq in seqs:
            if seq is None or seq.size != n:
                continue
            sc = score_normalized(seq)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = seq.copy()

            if len(top_candidates) < 10:
                top_candidates.append([sc, seq.copy()])
            else:
                max_idx = 0
                max_sc = top_candidates[0][0]
                for ii in range(1, len(top_candidates)):
                    if top_candidates[ii][0] > max_sc:
                        max_sc = top_candidates[ii][0]
                        max_idx = ii
                if sc < top_candidates[max_idx][0]:
                    top_candidates[max_idx] = [sc, seq.copy()]

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_power_left(1024, 0.5)
        best_score = score_normalized(best_a)

    main_a = best_a.copy()
    main_len = int(main_a.size)
    for item in top_candidates:
        sc, a = item
        sc = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score + 0.005 and int(a.size) > main_len and int(a.size) <= 4096:
            main_a = a.copy()
            main_len = int(a.size)

    print(f"Initial best: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}; main start n={main_len}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90 and best_a is not None:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.45, 700.0)
        if t1 > 30:
            a_new, _ = smooth_mirror(main_a, t1, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0,
                                     eta_init=0.8, verbose=True, label="main")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After main: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    if best_a is not None and best_a.size < 8192:
        bl = int(best_a.size)
        targets = []
        for t in (1536, 2048, 3072, 4096, 8192):
            if t > bl:
                targets.append(t)
        if len(targets) > 2:
            targets = targets[-2:]

        for target in targets:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 90:
                break
            if target >= 8192:
                t_slice = min(rem * 0.16, 180.0)
            elif target >= 4096:
                t_slice = min(rem * 0.18, 220.0)
            else:
                t_slice = min(rem * 0.18, 160.0)
            if t_slice < 30:
                break

            interp = interpolate_seq(best_a, target)
            starts = [
                interp,
                symmetrize(interp),
                make_power_left(target, 0.5),
                make_power_left_decay(target, 0.5, 0.25)
            ]
            start_best = interp
            start_sc = float("inf")
            for s in starts:
                sc = score_normalized(s)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < start_sc:
                    start_sc = sc
                    start_best = s

            a_new, _ = smooth_mirror(start_best, t_slice, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0,
                                     eta_init=0.8, verbose=False, label=f"long{target}")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After long {target}: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 60 and best_a is not None and best_a.size == target:
                t_add = min(rem * 0.05, 60.0)
                if t_add > 20:
                    a_add, _ = additive_mirror(best_a, t_add, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0,
                                              eta_init=0.5, verbose=False, label=f"longadd{target}")
                    b, sa = update_best(a_add, best_score, best_a)
                    best_score, best_a = b, sa

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120 and best_a is not None:
        t2 = min(rem * 0.15, 240.0)
        if t2 > 30:
            a_new, _ = additive_mirror(best_a, t2, p_start=4.0, p_max=128.0,
                                       eta_init=0.6, verbose=False, label="add")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After additive: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90 and best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 4096:
        t3 = min(rem * 0.10, 120.0)
        if t3 > 30:
            a_new, _ = newton_flatten(best_a, t3, reg_frac=1e-7, verbose=False, label="newton")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After newton: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90 and best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 2048:
        t4 = min(rem * 0.15, 180.0)
        if t4 > 30:
            a_new, _ = coordinate_refine(best_a, t4, verbose=False, label="coord")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After coord: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30 and best_a is not None:
        t5 = min(rem - 20.0, 300.0)
        if t5 > 20:
            a_new, _ = smooth_mirror(best_a, t5, p_start=8.0, p_max=256.0,
                                     eta_init=0.3, verbose=True, label="final")
            b, sa = update_best(a_new, best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            b, sa = update_best(symmetrize(a_new), best_score, best_a)
            best_score, best_a = b, sa
            print(f"After final polish: {best_score:.6f}, n={int(best_a.size)}")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_power_left(1024, 0.5)

    best_a = normalize_nonnegative(best_a)
    sym_a = symmetrize(best_a)

    final_a = best_a
    final_score = exact_score(best_a)
    sym_score = exact_score(sym_a)
    if sym_score < final_score:
        final_a = sym_a
        final_score = sym_score

    final_a = normalize_nonnegative(final_a)
    final_score = exact_score(final_a)

    print(f"FINAL exact score: {final_score:.6f}, n={int(final_a.size)}")
    return [float(x) for x in final_a]
```
